{/* Generated by scripts/docs-components.mjs. Do not edit. */}

Each component below is drawn with the Pretex[t] brand kit's style. Copy the React file and its CSS into your project. The look is in the custom properties at the top of the CSS.

**Accessibility:** 55 of 57 components pass an automated scan with axe-core 4.14.0 against WCAG 2.2 AA. Each component is scanned in a browser as drawn here, at rest. Hover and focus states, keyboard use and screen reader output still need a person to check.

## Button · Primary

<PreviewButtonPrimary />

<Check>
**Accessibility: no issues found.** axe-core 4.14.0 passed 4 WCAG 2.2 AA checks on this component.
</Check>

<CodeGroup>
```tsx ButtonPrimary.tsx
import type * as React from "react";
import "./ButtonPrimary.css";

interface ButtonPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  onClick?: () => void;
}

/** Primary button, from the brand kit. */
export function ButtonPrimary({ className, children = "Primary", onClick }: ButtonPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <button className={className ? `button-primary ${className}` : "button-primary"} type="button" onClick={onClick}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}
```

```css ButtonPrimary.css
.button-primary {
  --button-primary-background: var(--kit-hnp52d, #3f69db);
  --button-primary-color: #f8f9f9;
  --button-primary-font-family: "General Sans", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --button-primary-font-size: 15px;
  --button-primary-font-weight: 500;
  --button-primary-line-height: 1;
  --button-primary-padding: 8px 18px;
  --button-primary-radius: 32px;
  --button-primary-border: 1px solid var(--kit-hnp52d, #3f69db);
  --button-primary-shadow: 0px 0px 0px 2px color-mix(in srgb, var(--kit-hnp52d, #3f69db) 48%, transparent);
  --button-primary-icon-size: 17px;
  --button-primary-hover-background: #486dca;
  --button-primary-focus-ring-color: currentColor;
  --button-primary-focus-ring-width: 2px;

  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: var(--button-primary-background);
  color: var(--button-primary-color);
  font-family: var(--button-primary-font-family);
  font-size: var(--button-primary-font-size);
  font-weight: var(--button-primary-font-weight);
  line-height: var(--button-primary-line-height);
  padding: var(--button-primary-padding);
  border-radius: var(--button-primary-radius);
  border: var(--button-primary-border);
  box-shadow: var(--button-primary-shadow);
}

.button-primary { cursor: pointer; }
.button-primary:hover {
  background: var(--button-primary-hover-background, var(--button-primary-background));
  color: var(--button-primary-hover-color, var(--button-primary-color));
  border-color: var(--button-primary-hover-border-color, currentColor);
  box-shadow: var(--button-primary-hover-shadow, var(--button-primary-shadow));
}
.button-primary:focus-visible {
  outline: var(--button-primary-focus-ring-width, 2px) solid var(--button-primary-focus-ring-color, currentColor);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}
```

```tsx CardPrimary.tsx
import type * as React from "react";
import "./CardPrimary.css";

interface CardPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  title?: string;
}

/** Primary card, from the brand kit. */
export function CardPrimary({ className, children = "Supporting copy goes here.", title = "Primary" }: CardPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <article className={className ? `card-primary ${className}` : "card-primary"}>
      <div className="card-primary__slots">
        <div className="card-primary__slot" data-slot="header" data-led>
          <div className="card-primary__lead card-primary__text">
            <strong className="card-primary__title">{title}</strong>
            <p className="card-primary__body">{children}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

```css CardPrimary.css
.card-primary {
  --card-primary-background: var(--kit-hqrhrk, #f6f4f1);
  --card-primary-color: #000000;
  --card-primary-font-family: "General Sans", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --card-primary-font-size: 16px;
  --card-primary-font-weight: 500;
  --card-primary-line-height: 1.3;
  --card-primary-padding: 16px 16px;
  --card-primary-radius: 8px;
  --card-primary-border: 1px solid var(--kit-17e2dceb-2877-475d-a85c-c2a066a2bded, #d3d3d0);
  --card-primary-shadow: 0px 0px 0px 2px color-mix(in srgb, #000000 5%, transparent);
  --card-primary-body-size: 14px;
  --card-primary-title-weight: 600;
  --card-primary-icon-color: #486dca;
  --card-primary-icon-size: 20px;
  --card-primary-focus-ring-color: currentColor;
  --card-primary-focus-ring-width: 2px;

  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: var(--card-primary-background);
  color: var(--card-primary-color);
  font-family: var(--card-primary-font-family);
  font-size: var(--card-primary-font-size);
  font-weight: var(--card-primary-font-weight);
  line-height: var(--card-primary-line-height);
  padding: var(--card-primary-padding);
  border-radius: var(--card-primary-radius);
  border: var(--card-primary-border);
  box-shadow: var(--card-primary-shadow);
}

.card-primary { display: grid; gap: 6px; align-content: start; width: 100%; min-width: 0;  }
.card-primary:hover {
  background: var(--card-primary-hover-background, var(--card-primary-background));
  color: var(--card-primary-hover-color, var(--card-primary-color));
  border-color: var(--card-primary-hover-border-color, currentColor);
  box-shadow: var(--card-primary-hover-shadow, var(--card-primary-shadow));
}
.card-primary__text { display: grid; gap: 6px; align-content: start; }
.card-primary__icon { color: var(--card-primary-icon-color, currentColor); }
.card-primary__title { font-weight: var(--card-primary-title-weight, bolder); }
.card-primary__body { margin: 0; white-space: pre-line; opacity: 0.8; font-size: var(--card-primary-body-size, inherit); }
.card-primary__slots { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.card-primary__slots:not([data-dividers]) { gap: 12px; }
.card-primary__slots[data-dividers] > .card-primary__slot + .card-primary__slot { border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 14%, transparent); }
.card-primary__slot, .card-primary__items { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.card-primary__items { align-items: flex-start; }
.card-primary__slot[data-led] { flex-direction: row; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; }
.card-primary__slot[data-led] > .card-primary__items { flex: 0 0 auto; }
.card-primary__items[data-layout="row"] { flex-direction: row; align-items: center; }
.card-primary__items[data-layout="grid"] { display: grid; }
.card-primary__items[data-fill] > * { align-self: stretch; width: auto; min-width: 0; }
.card-primary__items[data-fill][data-layout="row"] > :not([data-flex-item]) { flex: 1 1 0; }
.card-primary__slot[data-led] > .card-primary__items[data-fill] { flex: 1 1 0; }
.card-primary__lead { flex: 1 1 8rem; min-width: 0; }
```
</CodeGroup>

```tsx Dialog.tsx
/** Dialog dialog, from the brand kit. */
export function Dialog({ className, children = "Dialog" }: DialogProps) {
  return (
    <div className={className ? `dialog ${className}` : "dialog"} role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Dialog">
      <strong className="dialog__title">{children}</strong>
      <p className="dialog__body">Supporting details and a clear next step.</p>
      <div className="dialog__actions">
        <button type="button">Cancel</button>
        <button type="button">Confirm</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css Dialog.css
.dialog {
  --dialog-background: var(--kit-hqrhrk, #f6f4f1);
  --dialog-color: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #000609);

  font-size: var(--dialog-font-size);
  font-weight: var(--dialog-font-weight);
  line-height: var(--dialog-line-height);
  padding: var(--dialog-padding);
  border-radius: var(--dialog-radius);
  border: var(--dialog-border);
  box-shadow: var(--dialog-shadow);
}

.dialog { display: grid; gap: 12px; width: min(100%, 24rem); }
.dialog { box-shadow: 0 12px 32px color-mix(in srgb, #000 20%, transparent); }
.dialog__body { margin: 0; }
.dialog__actions { display: flex; gap: 8px; justify-content: end; }
.dialog__actions > button { padding: 0; font: inherit; color: inherit; cursor: pointer; background: none; border: 0; }
```

```tsx MenuDropdownMenu.tsx
import type * as React from "react";

interface MenuDropdownMenuProps {

}

/** Dropdown Menu menu, from the brand kit. */
export function MenuDropdownMenu({ className, children = "Dropdown Menu" }: MenuDropdownMenuProps) {

        </svg>
      </summary>

        <button className="menu-dropdown-menu__item" role="menuitem" type="button">Rename</button>
        <button className="menu-dropdown-menu__item" role="menuitem" type="button">Duplicate</button>
        <button className="menu-dropdown-menu__item" role="menuitem" type="button">Delete</button>

  );
}
```

```css MenuDropdownMenu.css
.menu-dropdown-menu__list { position: absolute; z-index: 10; top: calc(100% + 4px); left: 0; display: grid; min-width: 100%; padding: 4px; background: var(--kit-hqrhrk, #f6f4f1); color: var(--menu-dropdown-menu-color); border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--menu-dropdown-menu-color) 15%, transparent); border-radius: var(--menu-dropdown-menu-radius); box-shadow: 0 8px 24px color-mix(in srgb, #000 16%, transparent); }
.menu-dropdown-menu__item { all: unset; padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: calc(var(--menu-dropdown-menu-radius) - 2px); cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap; }
.menu-dropdown-menu__item:hover, .menu-dropdown-menu__item:focus-visible { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--menu-dropdown-menu-color) 10%, transparent); }
```

```tsx SelectPrimary.tsx
import { Select } from "@base-ui/react/select";
import "./SelectPrimary.css";

interface SelectPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  onValueChange?: (value: string | null) => void;
  options?: string[];
  placeholder?: string;
  value?: string | null;
}

/** SelectPrimary: requires @base-ui/react for its accessible dropdown. */
export function SelectPrimary({ className, onValueChange, options = ["Overview","Settings","Activity"], placeholder = "Primary", value }: SelectPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <Select.Root defaultValue={placeholder ? null : "Choose an option"} value={value} onValueChange={onValueChange}>
      <div className={className ? `select-primary ${className}` : "select-primary"}>
        <Select.Trigger className="select-primary__trigger" aria-label={placeholder || "Choose an option"}>
          <Select.Value placeholder={placeholder} />
          <Select.Icon><svg className="select-primary__chevron" width={14} height={14} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
  <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />
</svg></Select.Icon>
        </Select.Trigger>
      </div>
      <Select.Portal>
        <Select.Positioner className="select-primary__positioner" sideOffset={4} alignItemWithTrigger={false}>
          <Select.Popup className="select-primary__list">
            <Select.List>
              {options.map((option) => (
                <Select.Item className="select-primary__item" key={option} value={option}>
                  <Select.ItemText>{option}</Select.ItemText>
                </Select.Item>
              ))}
            </Select.List>
          </Select.Popup>
        </Select.Positioner>
      </Select.Portal>
    </Select.Root>
  );
}
```

## Tabs · Primary

<PreviewTabsPrimary />

<Check>
**Accessibility: no issues found.** axe-core 4.14.0 passed 10 WCAG 2.2 AA checks on this component.
</Check>

<CodeGroup>
```tsx TabsPrimary.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import "./TabsPrimary.css";

interface TabsPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  onChange?: (tab: string) => void;
  tabs?: string[];
  value?: string;
}

/** Tabs from the brand kit: the open one is marked, and a click moves it. */
export function TabsPrimary({
  className,
  onChange,
  tabs = ["Overview","Settings","Activity"],
  value = "Overview",
}: TabsPrimaryProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(value);
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Tabs" className={className ? `tabs-primary ${className}` : "tabs-primary"}>
      {tabs.map((tab) => (
        <button
          aria-current={tab === open ? "page" : undefined}
          className="tabs-primary__tab"
          key={tab}
          onClick={() => {
            setOpen(tab);
            onChange?.(tab);
          }}
          type="button"
        >
          {tab}
        </button>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}
```


```css TablePrimary.css
.table-primary__button[data-style="solid"] { color: #fff; background: var(--table-primary-tint); }
.table-primary__button[data-style="outline"] { color: var(--table-primary-tint); background: transparent; }
.table-primary__progress { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.35em; width: 100%; min-inline-size: 8rem; }
.table-primary__progress small { font-size: inherit; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }

.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(2) > td:nth-child(2) :is(.table-primary__tag, .table-primary__chip, .table-primary__button, .table-primary__progress) { background: var(--kit-yr89bp, #a8a6a4); border: 1px solid var(--kit-yr89bp, #a8a6a4); border-radius: 6px; color: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #000609); font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; line-height: 1; padding-block: 4px; padding-inline: 10px; }
```

```tsx ToastPrimary.tsx
import type * as React from "react";
import "./ToastPrimary.css";

interface ToastPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

/** Primary toast, from the brand kit. */
export function ToastPrimary({ className, children = "Primary" }: ToastPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <div className={className ? `toast-primary ${className}` : "toast-primary"} role="status">
      <span className="toast-primary__text">{children}</span>
      <button className="toast-primary__action" type="button">Undo</button>
      <button className="toast-primary__close" type="button" aria-label="Close">
        <svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M18 6 6 18M6 6l12 12" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" />
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx PanelPrimary.tsx
import { Collapsible } from "@base-ui/react/collapsible";
import "./PanelPrimary.css";

type PanelPrimaryProps = Pick<Collapsible.Root.Props, "open" | "defaultOpen" | "onOpenChange"> & { className?: string; toggleSide?: "left" | "right" };

export function PanelPrimary({ className, toggleSide = "right", defaultOpen = true, ...props }: PanelPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <Collapsible.Root {...props} defaultOpen={defaultOpen} data-toggle-side={toggleSide} aria-label="Primary" className={["panel-primary", className].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}>
      <Collapsible.Panel className="panel-primary__inner">
        <div className="panel-primary__surface">
<div className="panel-primary__slots" data-dividers>
  <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="header" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 16px" }}>
    <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center" }}>
    <strong className="panel-primary__lead">Primary</strong>
      <span className="panel-primary__header-1">
        3
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="body" style={{ gap: 12, padding: "16px 16px" }}>
    <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 12 }}>
      <div className="panel-primary__body-1">
        <span className="panel-primary__body-1__label">
          <span>Review progress</span>
          <span>60%</span>
        </span>
        <span className="panel-primary__body-1__track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Review progress" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={60}>
          <span className="panel-primary__body-1__fill" style={{ inlineSize: "60%" }} />
        </span>
      </div>
      <label className="panel-primary__body-2">
        <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked />
        Select all
      </label>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="footer" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 16px" }}>
    <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
      <button className="panel-primary__footer-1" type="button">
        + New item
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</div>
      </Collapsible.Panel>
      <div className="panel-primary__hide">
<Collapsible.Trigger className="panel-primary__trigger" aria-label="Toggle Primary" /><div inert aria-hidden="true"><button className="panel-primary__hide-1" type="button" aria-label="Arrow Left">
  <svg className="panel-primary__hide-1__icon" width={16} height={16} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
    <path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" />
  </svg>
</button></div>
</div>
    </Collapsible.Root>
  );
}
```

```tsx NavbarPrimary.tsx
import type * as React from "react";
import "./NavbarPrimary.css";

interface NavbarPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
}

/** Primary navbar, from the brand kit. */
export function NavbarPrimary({ className }: NavbarPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <nav className={className ? `navbar-primary ${className}` : "navbar-primary"} aria-label="Navigation">
      <div className="navbar-primary__slots">
        <div className="navbar-primary__slot" data-slot="header" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 20px" }}>
          <div className="navbar-primary__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center" }}>
            <img className="navbar-primary__header-1" src="https://ftxhendy6jwk0sx5.public.blob.vercel-storage.com/boards/frames/frame%402x-Gj6faYmsxd4lA09n8tmwH7FR8znJiw.png" alt="Logo" />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="navbar-primary__slot" data-slot="body" style={{ gap: 24, padding: "12px 0px" }}>
          <div className="navbar-primary__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 24, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}>
            <a className="navbar-primary__body-1" href="#">
              Product
            </a>
            <a className="navbar-primary__body-2" href="#">
              Pricing
            </a>
            <a className="navbar-primary__body-3" href="#">
              About
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="navbar-primary__slot" data-slot="footer" style={{ gap: 12, padding: "12px 20px" }}>
          <div className="navbar-primary__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 12, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center" }}>
            <a className="navbar-primary__footer-1" href="#">
              User Name
            </a>
            <span className="navbar-primary__footer-2" role="img" aria-label={"DP"}>
              <img src={"https://ik.imagekit.io/dtunrco/yy5Rr074J_Kjn0J-Znr1ixWJPXHFgMplhimgCAxcSpA.jpg"} alt={"DP"} />
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx AvatarPrimary.tsx
import { Avatar } from "@base-ui/react/avatar";

    </Avatar.Root>
  );
}
```

```tsx AccordionPrimary.tsx
import { Accordion } from "@base-ui/react/accordion";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import "./AccordionPrimary.css";

type AccordionPrimaryProps = { title?: string; summary?: ReactNode; details?: ReactNode; icon?: ReactNode; leadingIcon?: ReactNode; defaultOpen?: boolean };

export function AccordionPrimary({ title = "Primary", summary = (<div className="accordion-primary__slots">

</div>), details = (<div className="accordion-primary__slots">
  <div className="accordion-primary__slot" data-slot="body" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "0px 0px" }}>
    <div className="accordion-primary__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 8 }}>
      <label className="accordion-primary__body-1">
        Details
      </label>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>), leadingIcon = null, icon = (<svg className="accordion-primary__icon" width={14} height={14} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
  <path d="m5 9 7 7 7-7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" />
</svg>), defaultOpen = false }: AccordionPrimaryProps) {
  return <Accordion.Root className="accordion-primary" defaultValue={defaultOpen ? ["details"] : []}>
    <Accordion.Item value="details">
      <div className="accordion-primary__summary">
        <Accordion.Header className="accordion-primary__disclosure"><Accordion.Trigger aria-label={title || "Toggle details"} className="accordion-primary__trigger"><span className="accordion-primary__label">{leadingIcon}{title}</span>{icon && <span className="accordion-primary__indicator">{icon}</span>}</Accordion.Trigger></Accordion.Header>
        {summary}
      </div>
      <Accordion.Panel>{details}</Accordion.Panel>
    </Accordion.Item>
  </Accordion.Root>;
}
```

```css AccordionPrimary.css
.accordion-primary {
  --accordion-primary-background: var(--kit-xoaop8, #fffefc);
  --accordion-primary-color: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #000609);
  --accordion-primary-font-family: "General Sans", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --accordion-primary-font-size: 14px;
  --accordion-primary-font-weight: 500;
  --accordion-primary-line-height: 1.25;
  --accordion-primary-padding: 8px 14px;
  --accordion-primary-radius: 6px;
  --accordion-primary-border: 1px solid var(--kit-17e2dceb-2877-475d-a85c-c2a066a2bded, #d3d3d0);
  --accordion-primary-shadow: 0px 0px 0px 2px color-mix(in srgb, var(--kit-18u0jwu, #000609) 3%, transparent);
  --accordion-primary-focus-ring-color: currentColor;
  --accordion-primary-focus-ring-width: 2px;

  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: var(--accordion-primary-background);
  color: var(--accordion-primary-color);
  font-family: var(--accordion-primary-font-family);
  font-size: var(--accordion-primary-font-size);
  font-weight: var(--accordion-primary-font-weight);
  line-height: var(--accordion-primary-line-height);
  padding: var(--accordion-primary-padding);
  border-radius: var(--accordion-primary-radius);
  border: var(--accordion-primary-border);
  box-shadow: var(--accordion-primary-shadow);
}

.accordion-primary { width: 100%; min-width: 0; }
.accordion-primary__summary { display: grid; grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0, 1fr) auto; align-items: center; gap: .5em; }
.accordion-primary__summary > :not(.accordion-primary__disclosure) { position: relative; grid-area: 1 / 2; }
.accordion-primary__label { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: .5em; text-align: start; }
.accordion-primary__indicator { display: inline-flex; grid-column: 3; }
.accordion-primary__disclosure { display: grid; grid-template-columns: subgrid; grid-area: 1 / 1 / 2 / -1; margin: 0; font: inherit; }
.accordion-primary__trigger { width: 100%; min-height: 1.5em; display: grid; grid-template-columns: subgrid; grid-column: 1 / -1; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: .5em; font: inherit; color: inherit; border: 0; background: transparent; cursor: pointer; }
.accordion-primary__trigger:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid currentColor; outline-offset: 2px; }
.accordion-primary__trigger[aria-expanded="true"] > .accordion-primary__indicator, .accordion-primary__disclosure[open] > summary > .accordion-primary__indicator { rotate: 180deg; }
.accordion-primary__icon { color: var(--accordion-primary-icon-color, currentColor); }
.accordion-primary__summary:has(> details:not([open])) + .accordion-primary__details { display: none; }
.accordion-primary__slots { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.accordion-primary__slots:not([data-dividers]) { gap: 12px; }
.accordion-primary__slots[data-dividers] > .accordion-primary__slot + .accordion-primary__slot { border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 14%, transparent); }
.accordion-primary__slot, .accordion-primary__items { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.accordion-primary__items { align-items: flex-start; }
.accordion-primary__slot[data-led] { flex-direction: row; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; }
.accordion-primary__slot[data-led] > .accordion-primary__items { flex: 0 0 auto; }
.accordion-primary__items[data-layout="row"] { flex-direction: row; align-items: center; }
.accordion-primary__items[data-layout="grid"] { display: grid; }
.accordion-primary__items[data-fill] > * { align-self: stretch; width: auto; min-width: 0; }
.accordion-primary__items[data-fill][data-layout="row"] > :not([data-flex-item]) { flex: 1 1 0; }
.accordion-primary__slot[data-led] > .accordion-primary__items[data-fill] { flex: 1 1 0; }
.accordion-primary__lead { flex: 1 1 8rem; min-width: 0; }

.accordion-primary__body-1 {
  --accordion-primary__body-1-background: transparent;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-color: var(--kit-l6h2ze, #00222d);
  --accordion-primary__body-1-font-family: "General Sans", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-font-size: 14px;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-font-weight: 600;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-line-height: 1.25;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-padding: 0px 0px;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-radius: 0px;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-border: none;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-shadow: none;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-focus-ring-color: currentColor;
  --accordion-primary__body-1-focus-ring-width: 2px;

  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: var(--accordion-primary__body-1-background);
  color: var(--accordion-primary__body-1-color);
  font-family: var(--accordion-primary__body-1-font-family);
  font-size: var(--accordion-primary__body-1-font-size);
  font-weight: var(--accordion-primary__body-1-font-weight);
  line-height: var(--accordion-primary__body-1-line-height);
  padding: var(--accordion-primary__body-1-padding);
  border-radius: var(--accordion-primary__body-1-radius);
  border: var(--accordion-primary__body-1-border);

}
```

```tsx PanelWorkspacePanel.tsx
import { Collapsible } from "@base-ui/react/collapsible";
import "./PanelWorkspacePanel.css";

type PanelWorkspacePanelProps = Pick<Collapsible.Root.Props, "open" | "defaultOpen" | "onOpenChange"> & { className?: string; toggleSide?: "left" | "right" };

export function PanelWorkspacePanel({ className, toggleSide = "left", defaultOpen = true, ...props }: PanelWorkspacePanelProps) {
  return (
    <Collapsible.Root {...props} defaultOpen={defaultOpen} data-toggle-side={toggleSide} aria-label="Workspace Panel" className={["panel-workspace-panel", className].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}>
      <Collapsible.Panel className="panel-workspace-panel__inner">
        <div className="panel-workspace-panel__surface">
<div className="panel-workspace-panel__slots">
  <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="header" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 16px" }}>
    <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 8, alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
    <strong className="panel-workspace-panel__lead">Workspace Panel</strong>
      <span className="panel-workspace-panel__header-1">
        3
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="body" style={{ gap: 10, padding: "0px 16px" }}>
    <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 10 }}>
      <div className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1">
        <span className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1__label">
          <span>Review progress</span>
          <span>60%</span>
        </span>
        <span className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1__track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Review progress" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={60}>
          <span className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1__fill" style={{ inlineSize: "60%" }} />
        </span>
      </div>
      <label className="panel-workspace-panel__body-2">
        <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked />
        Select all
      </label>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="footer" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 16px" }}>
    <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 8, alignItems: "stretch" }}>
      <button className="panel-workspace-panel__footer-1" type="button">
        + New item
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</div>
      </Collapsible.Panel>
      <div className="panel-workspace-panel__hide">
<Collapsible.Trigger className="panel-workspace-panel__trigger" aria-label="Toggle Workspace Panel" /><div inert aria-hidden="true"><button className="panel-workspace-panel__hide-1" type="button" aria-label="Arrow Left">
  <svg className="panel-workspace-panel__hide-1__icon" width={16} height={16} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
    <path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" />
  </svg>
</button></div>
</div>
    </Collapsible.Root>
  );
}
```

import { useState } from "react";

export const PreviewButtonPrimary = () => (
  <div className="not-prose bk-preview bk-pretex-t">
    <style>{"@import url(\"https://api.fontshare.com/v2/css?f[]=general-sans%40100%2C200%2C300%2C400%2C500%2C600%2C700%2C800%2C900&display=swap\");\n/* Generated by scripts/docs-components.mjs. Do not edit. */\n.bk-preview {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  align-items: flex-start;\n  gap: 1rem;\n  padding: 2rem;\n  margin: 1rem 0;\n  border: 1px solid rgb(127 127 127 / 0.2);\n  border-radius: 0.75rem;\n  background: #fff;\n  overflow-x: auto;\n  /* A light box on a dark page: native controls (a select's list, a\n     checkbox) draw light, as they do where the kit is used. */\n  color-scheme: light;\n}\n\n/*\n * The docs theme stops at the preview.       <div className="panel-primary__slots" data-dividers>
        <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="header" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 16px" }}>
          <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center" }}>
          <strong className="panel-primary__lead">Primary</strong>
            <span className="panel-primary__header-1">
              3
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="body" style={{ gap: 12, padding: "16px 16px" }}>
          <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 12 }}>
            <div className="panel-primary__body-1">
              <span className="panel-primary__body-1__label">
                <span>Review progress</span>
                <span>60%</span>
              </span>
              <span className="panel-primary__body-1__track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Review progress" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={60}>
                <span className="panel-primary__body-1__fill" style={{ inlineSize: "60%" }} />
              </span>
            </div>
            <label className="panel-primary__body-2">
              <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked />
              Select all
            </label>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="footer" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 16px" }}>
          <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
            <button className="panel-primary__footer-1" type="button">
              + New item
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      </div></div>
      <div className="panel-primary__hide">
      <button type="button" className="panel-primary__trigger" aria-label="Toggle Primary" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} /><div inert aria-hidden="true"><button className="panel-primary__hide-1" type="button" aria-label="Arrow Left">
        <svg className="panel-primary__hide-1__icon" width={16} height={16} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" />
        </svg>
      </button></div>
      </div>
    </section>
  </div>
);
};

    <section className="panel-workspace-panel" aria-label="Workspace Panel" data-toggle-side="left">
      <div className="panel-workspace-panel__inner" data-panel-open={open} inert={!open}><div className="panel-workspace-panel__surface">
      <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slots">
        <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="header" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 16px" }}>
          <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 8, alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
          <strong className="panel-workspace-panel__lead">Workspace Panel</strong>
            <span className="panel-workspace-panel__header-1">
              3
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="body" style={{ gap: 10, padding: "0px 16px" }}>
          <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 10 }}>
            <div className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1">
              <span className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1__label">
                <span>Review progress</span>
                <span>60%</span>
              </span>
              <span className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1__track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Review progress" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={60}>
                <span className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1__fill" style={{ inlineSize: "60%" }} />
              </span>
            </div>
            <label className="panel-workspace-panel__body-2">
              <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked />
              Select all
            </label>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="footer" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 16px" }}>
          <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 8, alignItems: "stretch" }}>
            <button className="panel-workspace-panel__footer-1" type="button">
              + New item
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      </div></div>
      <div className="panel-workspace-panel__hide">
      <button type="button" className="panel-workspace-panel__trigger" aria-label="Toggle Workspace Panel" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} /><div inert aria-hidden="true"><button className="panel-workspace-panel__hide-1" type="button" aria-label="Arrow Left">
        <svg className="panel-workspace-panel__hide-1__icon" width={16} height={16} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" />
        </svg>
      </button></div>
      </div>
    </section>
  </div>
);
};
